  children: ReactNode
}

const RootLayout = ({ children }: RootLayoutProps) => {
  return (
    <html lang="en">
      <body>
        <Provider store={store}>
          <ErrorBoundary>
            {children}
          </ErrorBoundary>

// src/components/Common/Button.tsx

"use client"

import React from 'react'
import { cn } from '../../utils/helpers'

interface ButtonProps extends React.ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> {
  variant?: 'primary' | 'secondary'
}

export const Button: React.FC<ButtonProps> = ({ variant = 'primary', className, children, ...props }) => {
  const baseStyles = "px-3 py-2 rounded-md focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 inline-flex items-center text-sm font-medium"
  const variantStyles = variant === 'primary' 
    ? "bg-blue-600 text-white hover:bg-blue-700 focus:ring-blue-500"
    : "bg-gray-200 text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-300 focus:ring-gray-400"

  return (
    <button className={cn(baseStyles, variantStyles, className)} {...props}>
      {children}
    </button>
  )
}

// src/components/Common/Progress.tsx

"use client"

import React from 'react'

interface ProgressProps {
  value: number
  max: number
}

export const Progress: React.FC<ProgressProps> = ({ value, max }) => {
  const percentage = Math.min((value / max) * 100, 100)

  return (
    <div className="w-full bg-gray-200 rounded-full h-2.5 dark:bg-gray-700">
      <div
        className="bg-blue-600 h-2.5 rounded-full"
        style={{ width: `${percentage}%` }}
      ></div>
    </div>
  )
}

File: raay/src/components/Common/Select.tsx
================
// src/components/Common/Select.tsx

import React from 'react';

interface SelectOption {
  value: string;
  label: string;
}

interface SelectProps {
  value: string;
  onChange: (event: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLSelectElement>) => void;
  options: SelectOption[];
  placeholder?: string;
}

export const Select: React.FC<SelectProps> = ({ value, onChange, options, placeholder }) => {
  return (
    <select
      value={value}
      onChange={onChange}
      className="w-full p-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-md shadow-sm focus:border-indigo-300 focus:ring focus:ring-indigo-200 focus:ring-opacity-50"
    >
      {placeholder && (
        <option value="" disabled>
          {placeholder}
        </option>
      )}
      {options.map((option) => (
        <option key={option.value} value={option.value}>
          {option.label}
        </option>
      ))}
    </select>
  );
};

================
File: raay/src/components/Common/ThemeToggle.tsx
================
// src/components/Common/ThemeToggle.tsx

"use client"

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { SunIcon, MoonIcon } from '@heroicons/react/24/outline'

const ThemeToggle: React.FC = () => {
  const [isDark, setIsDark] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isDark) {
      document.documentElement.classList.add('dark')
    } else {
      document.documentElement.classList.remove('dark')
    }
  }, [isDark])

  return (
    <button onClick={() => setIsDark(!isDark)} className="p-2 rounded-md focus:outline-none">
      {isDark ? <SunIcon className="h-6 w-6 text-yellow-400" /> : <MoonIcon className="h-6 w-6 text-gray-800" />}
    </button>
  )
}

export default ThemeToggle

// src/components/Common/Tooltip.tsx

import React from 'react'
import { Transition } from '@headlessui/react'

export interface TooltipProps {
  content: string
  children: React.ReactNode
  visible?: boolean
}

export const Tooltip: React.FC<TooltipProps> = ({ content, children, visible = true }) => {
  return (
    <div className="relative">
      {children}
      <Transition
        show={visible}
        enter="transition ease-out duration-200"
        enterFrom="opacity-0 translate-y-1"
        enterTo="opacity-100 translate-y-0"
        leave="transition ease-in duration-150"
        leaveFrom="opacity-100 translate-y-0"
        leaveTo="opacity-0 translate-y-1"
      >
        <div className="absolute z-10 bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 mb-2 w-max">
          <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-lg shadow-lg ring-1 ring-black ring-opacity-5">
            <div className="bg-gray-700 p-2">
              <p className="text-sm text-white">{content}</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </Transition>
    </div>
  )
}

File: raay/src/components/Playground/Characters/CharacterCard.tsx

// src/components/Playground/CharacterCard.tsx
"use client"

import React, { useRef } from 'react'
import { useDrag } from 'react-dnd'
import { Card } from '../../Common/Card'

interface CharacterCardProps {
  character: {
    id: string
    name: string
    description: string
  }
}

interface DragItem {
  id: string
  type: string
}

const CharacterCard: React.FC<CharacterCardProps> = ({ character }) => {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
  const [{ isDragging }, drag] = useDrag<DragItem, void, { isDragging: boolean }>(() => ({
    type: 'CHARACTER',
    item: { id: character.id, type: 'CHARACTER' },
    collect: (monitor) => ({
      isDragging: !!monitor.isDragging(),
    }),
  }), [character.id])

  // Apply the drag ref to the div ref
  drag(ref)

  return (
    <div
      ref={ref}
      className={`bg-blue-100 p-2 rounded-md mb-2 cursor-pointer ${
        isDragging ? 'opacity-50' : 'opacity-100'
      }`}
    >
      <h5 className="text-sm font-medium">{character.name}</h5>
    </div>
  )
}

export default CharacterCard

File: raay/src/components/Playground/Characters/CharacterList.tsx
================
import React, { useRef } from 'react'
import { useSelector } from 'react-redux'
import { RootState } from '../../../store'
import { useDrag } from 'react-dnd'
import { UserIcon } from '@heroicons/react/24/outline'

interface CharacterItemProps {
  id: string
  name: string
}

const CharacterItem: React.FC<CharacterItemProps> = ({ id, name }) => {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
  const [{ isDragging }, drag] = useDrag(() => ({
    type: 'CHARACTER',
    item: { id, name },
    collect: (monitor) => ({
      isDragging: !!monitor.isDragging(),
    }),
  }))

  // Use drag to set up the ref
  drag(ref)

  return (
    <div
      ref={ref}
      className={`flex items-center p-2 mb-2 bg-white rounded-md shadow-sm cursor-move ${
        isDragging ? 'opacity-50' : ''
      }`}
    >
      <UserIcon className="h-5 w-5 mr-2 text-blue-500" />
      <span>{name}</span>
    </div>
  )
}

const CharacterList: React.FC = () => {
  const characters = useSelector((state: RootState) => state.characters.characters)

  return (
    <div className="mt-4">
      <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold mb-2">Characters</h3>
      <div className="space-y-2">
        {characters.map((character) => (
          <CharacterItem key={character.id} id={character.id} name={character.name} />
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

export default CharacterList

// src/components/Playground/PropItem.tsx

import React, { useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useDrag } from 'react-dnd';
import { Card } from '../../Common/Card';

interface PropItemProps {
  prop: {
    id: string;
    name: string;
    description: string;
  };
}

interface DragItem {
  id: string;
  type: string;
}

const PropItem: React.FC<PropItemProps> = ({ prop }) => {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  const [{ isDragging }, drag] = useDrag<DragItem, void, { isDragging: boolean }>(() => ({
    type: 'PROP',
    item: { id: prop.id, type: 'PROP' },
    collect: (monitor) => ({
      isDragging: monitor.isDragging(),
    }),
  }));

  useEffect(() => {
    if (ref.current) {
      drag(ref.current);
    }
  }, [drag]);

  return (
    <div
      ref={ref}
      className={`bg-green-100 p-2 rounded-md mb-2 cursor-pointer ${
        isDragging ? 'opacity-50' : 'opacity-100'
      }`}
    >
      <h5 className="text-sm font-medium">{prop.name}</h5>
    </div>
  );
};

export default PropItem;

================
File: raay/src/components/Playground/SceneBox/SceneBox.tsx
================
// src/components/Playground/SceneBox/SceneBox.tsx

import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useDispatch, useSelector } from 'react-redux';
import { 
  updateScene, 
  assignCharacterToScene, 
  deleteScene, 
  addScene 
} from '../../../store/scenesSlice';
import { RootState } from '../../../store';
import { Tooltip } from '../../Common/Tooltip';
import { UserIcon, PencilIcon, TrashIcon } from '@heroicons/react/24/outline';
import { useDrag, useDrop } from 'react-dnd';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { cn } from '../../../lib/utils';
import ScreenwritingEditor from '../ScreenwritingEditor';
import FloatingToolbox from '../Toolbox/FloatingToolbox';
import { setConnectionMode } from '../../../store/uiSlice';

interface SceneBoxProps {
  data: {
    scene: {
      id: string;
      title: string;
      scriptContent: string;
      position: { x: number; y: number };
      size: { width: number; height: number };
      characters: string[];
      color: string;
    };
    index: number;
  };
}

const SceneBox: React.FC<SceneBoxProps> = ({ data }) => {
  const dispatch = useDispatch();
  const { scene, index } = data;
  const characters = useSelector((state: RootState) =>
    state.characters.characters.filter(char => scene.characters.includes(char.id))
  );
  const connectionMode = useSelector((state: RootState) => state.ui.connectionMode);

  const [title, setTitle] = useState(scene.title);
  const [scriptContent, setScriptContent] = useState(scene.scriptContent);
  const [isEditing, setIsEditing] = useState(false);

  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const titleRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const contentRef = useRef<HTMLTextAreaElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    setTitle(scene.title);
    setScriptContent(scene.scriptContent);
  }, [scene.title, scene.scriptContent]);

  const [{ isDragging }, drag] = useDrag(() => ({
    type: 'SCENE',
    item: { id: scene.id, index },
    collect: (monitor) => ({
      isDragging: !!monitor.isDragging(),
    }),
  }), [scene.id, index]);

  const [{ isOver }, drop] = useDrop(() => ({
    accept: 'CHARACTER',
    drop: (item: { id: string }) => {
      dispatch(assignCharacterToScene({ sceneId: scene.id, characterId: item.id }))
    },
    collect: (monitor) => ({
      isOver: !!monitor.isOver(),
    }),
  }), [scene.id, dispatch]);

  // Combine drag and drop refs
  const dragDropRef = (node: HTMLDivElement | null) => {
    drag(drop(node));
  };

  const handleTitleChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    setTitle(e.target.value);
  };

  const handleContentChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLTextAreaElement>) => {
    setScriptContent(e.target.value);
  };

  const handleBlur = () => {
    dispatch(updateScene({ id: scene.id, title, scriptContent }));
  };

  const handleDelete = () => {
    dispatch(deleteScene(scene.id));
  };

  const handleEdit = () => {
    setIsEditing(true);
  };

  const handleCloseEdit = () => {
    setIsEditing(false);
  };

  const handleUpdateScene = (updatedScene: { title: string; scriptContent: string }) => {
    dispatch(updateScene({ id: scene.id, ...updatedScene }));
    setTitle(updatedScene.title);
    setScriptContent(updatedScene.scriptContent);
    setIsEditing(false);
  };

  // Toolbox action handlers
  const handleConnect = () => {
    // Connection handled via FloatingToolbox and connection mode
    // No action needed here
  };

  const handleAddCharacter = () => {
    // Implement add character functionality, e.g., opening a modal to select/add characters
    console.log('Add Character');
  };

  const handleDuplicate = () => {
    const newScene = { ...scene, id: `scene-${Date.now()}` };
    dispatch(addScene(newScene));
    console.log('Duplicate Scene');
  };

  const handleViewDetails = () => {
    // Implement view details functionality, e.g., opening a detailed view or modal
    console.log('View Details');
  };

  const handleAddComment = () => {
    // Implement add comment functionality, e.g., opening a comments sidebar or modal
    console.log('Add Comment');
  };

  return (
    <>
      <motion.div
        ref={dragDropRef}
        className={cn(
          "relative p-4 rounded-lg shadow-md border border-gray-300 transition-all duration-300 ease-in-out cursor-pointer group",
          isOver ? "border-blue-500 border-2" : "",
          connectionMode ? "ring-4 ring-blue-500" : ""
        )}
        style={{
          width: scene.size.width,
          height: scene.size.height,
          opacity: isDragging ? 0.5 : 1,
        }}
        onClick={() => {
          if (connectionMode) {
            dispatch(setConnectionMode(false));
          }
        }}
        animate={{ scale: 1 }}
        whileHover={{ scale: 1.02 }}
        whileTap={{ scale: 0.98 }}
      >
        <FloatingToolbox
          onEdit={handleEdit}
          onDelete={handleDelete}
          onAddCharacter={handleAddCharacter}
          onDuplicate={handleDuplicate}
          onViewDetails={handleViewDetails}
          onAddComment={handleAddComment}
          sceneId={scene.id}
        />

<div className="absolute top-0 left-0 right-0 p-0">
          <input
            ref={titleRef}
            type="text"
            value={title}
            onChange={handleTitleChange}
            onBlur={handleBlur}
            className="text-lg font-semibold bg-transparent border-none focus:outline-none focus:ring-0 w-full text-center"
            placeholder="Scene Title"
          />
        </div>
        <div className="absolute top-9 bottom-5 left-2 right-2">
          <textarea
            ref={contentRef}
            value={scriptContent}
            onChange={handleContentChange}
            onBlur={handleBlur}
            className="w-full h-full p-2 text-sm bg-transparent border-none rounded-md resize-none focus:outline-none focus:ring-0"
            placeholder="Describe your scene..."
          />
        </div>

        <div className="absolute bottom-0 right-2 flex items-center space-x-2">
          <div className="flex -space-x-1">
            {characters.slice(0, 3).map(char => (
              <Tooltip key={char.id} content={char.name}>
                <div className="w-6 h-6 bg-blue-200 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center border-2 border-white">
                  <UserIcon className="h-4 w-4 text-blue-600" />
                </div>
              </Tooltip>
            ))}
          </div>
          <span className="text-xs text-gray-500">
            {characters.length} {characters.length === 1 ? 'character' : 'characters'}
          </span>
        </div>
        </motion.div>
      {isEditing && (
        <ScreenwritingEditor
          scene={{ id: scene.id, title, scriptContent }}
          onSave={handleUpdateScene}
          onClose={handleCloseEdit}
        />
      )}
    </>
  );
};

export default SceneBox;

import { createSlice, PayloadAction } from '@reduxjs/toolkit';

interface SceneConnection {
  id: string;
  source: string;
  target: string;
}

interface SceneConnectionState {
  connections: SceneConnection[];
}

const initialState: SceneConnectionState = {
  connections: [],
};

const sceneConnectionsSlice = createSlice({
  name: 'sceneConnections',
  initialState,
  reducers: {
    addConnection: (state, action: PayloadAction<SceneConnection>) => {
      state.connections.push(action.payload);
    },
  },
});

export const { addConnection } = sceneConnectionsSlice.actions;
export default sceneConnectionsSlice.reducer;

================
File: raay/src/components/Playground/SceneLinking/SceneConnections/SceneConnector.tsx
================
import React, { useState, useCallback } from 'react';
import ReactFlow, {
  ReactFlowProvider,
  addEdge,
  Connection,
  Edge,
  Node,
  NodeTypes,
  EdgeTypes,
  OnNodesChange,
  OnEdgesChange,
  OnConnect,
  Background,
  Controls,
  applyNodeChanges,
  applyEdgeChanges,
} from 'reactflow';
import 'reactflow/dist/style.css';
import SceneNode from './SceneNode';
import SceneLink from './SceneLink';

const nodeTypes: NodeTypes = {
  scene: SceneNode,
};

const edgeTypes: EdgeTypes = {
  sceneLink: SceneLink,
};

const initialNodes: Node[] = [
  { id: '1', type: 'scene', position: { x: 0, y: 0 }, data: { id: '1', title: 'Scene 1' } },
  { id: '2', type: 'scene', position: { x: 250, y: 0 }, data: { id: '2', title: 'Scene 2' } },
];

const SceneConnector: React.FC = () => {
  const [nodes, setNodes] = useState<Node[]>(initialNodes);
  const [edges, setEdges] = useState<Edge[]>([]);

  const onNodesChange: OnNodesChange = useCallback(
    (changes) => setNodes((nds) => applyNodeChanges(changes, nds)),
    [setNodes]
  );

  const onEdgesChange: OnEdgesChange = useCallback(
    (changes) => setEdges((eds) => applyEdgeChanges(changes, eds)),
    [setEdges]
  );

  const onConnect: OnConnect = useCallback(
    (connection) => setEdges((eds) => addEdge(connection, eds)),
    [setEdges]
  );

  return (
    <div style={{ width: '100%', height: '500px' }}>
      <ReactFlowProvider>
        <ReactFlow
          nodes={nodes}
          edges={edges}
          onNodesChange={onNodesChange}
          onEdgesChange={onEdgesChange}
          onConnect={onConnect}
          nodeTypes={nodeTypes}
          edgeTypes={edgeTypes}
          fitView
        >
          <Background />
          <Controls />
        </ReactFlow>
      </ReactFlowProvider>
    </div>
  );
};

export default SceneConnector;

File: raay/src/components/Playground/SceneLinking/SceneConnections/SceneLink.tsx

import React from 'react';
import { EdgeProps, getBezierPath } from 'reactflow';

const SceneLink: React.FC<EdgeProps> = ({
  id,
  sourceX,
  sourceY,
  targetX,
  targetY,
  sourcePosition,
  targetPosition,
  style = {},
  data,
}) => {
  const [edgePath] = getBezierPath({
    sourceX,
    sourceY,
    sourcePosition,
    targetX,
    targetY,
    targetPosition,
  });

  return (
    <path
      id={id}
      style={style}
      className="react-flow__edge-path"
      d={edgePath}
      markerEnd={data?.markerEnd}
    />
  );
};

export default SceneLink;

// src/components/Playground/Toolbar.tsx
"use client"

import React from 'react'
import { useDispatch } from 'react-redux'
import { addScene } from '../../../store/scenesSlice'
import { addCharacter } from '../../../store/charactersSlice'
import { v4 as uuidv4 } from 'uuid'
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'

const Toolbar: React.FC = () => {
  const dispatch = useDispatch()
  const router = useRouter()

  const handleAddScene = () => {
    const newScene = {
      id: uuidv4(),
      title: `Scene ${uuidv4().slice(0, 4)}`,
      scriptContent: '',
      position: { x: Math.random() * 500, y: Math.random() * 500 },
      size: { width: 250, height: 150 },
      characters: [],
      props: [],
      color: 'bg-white',
    }
    dispatch(addScene(newScene))
  }

  const handleAddCharacter = () => {
    const newCharacter = {
      id: uuidv4(),
      name: `Character ${uuidv4().slice(0, 4)}`,
      description: '',
    }
    dispatch(addCharacter(newCharacter))
  }

  const navigateToScreenplay = () => {
    router.push('/screenplay')
  }

  return (
    <div className="fixed top-0 left-0 w-full z-20 p-4">
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center font-sans text-black text-sm">
        <div className="flex space-x-4">
          <button onClick={handleAddScene} className="hover:text-gray-600 transition-colors duration-300 font-bold">
            [ADD SCENE]
          </button>
          <button onClick={handleAddCharacter} className="hover:text-gray-600 transition-colors duration-300 font-bold">
            [ADD CHARACTER]
          </button>
          <button onClick={handleAddCharacter} className="hover:text-gray-600 transition-colors duration-300 font-bold">
            [STORYBOARD]
          </button>          
        </div>
        <div className="flex space-x-6">
          <button onClick={navigateToScreenplay} className="hover:text-gray-600 transition-colors duration-300 font-bold">
            [VIEW SCREENPLAY]
          </button>
          <button className="hover:text-gray-600 transition-colors duration-300 font-bold">
            [ACCOUNT]
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

// src/components/Playground/Toolbox/ConnectionTypeToolbox.tsx

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useDispatch, useSelector } from 'react-redux';
import { RootState } from '../../../store';
import { setActiveConnectionType } from '../../../store/uiSlice';
import { ConnectionType } from '../../../store/connectionsSlice';
import { PencilIcon, TrashIcon, LinkIcon, UserPlusIcon, FireIcon, DocumentDuplicateIcon, ChatBubbleBottomCenterIcon } from '@heroicons/react/24/outline';
import { Tooltip } from '../../Common/Tooltip';

interface ConnectionTypeToolboxProps {
  isVisible: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
}

const connectionTypes: { type: ConnectionType; icon: React.ElementType; label: string }[] = [
  { type: 'sequential', icon: LinkIcon, label: 'Sequential' },
  { type: 'nonLinear', icon: PencilIcon, label: 'Non-Linear' },
  { type: 'reference', icon: UserPlusIcon, label: 'Reference' },
  { type: 'arc', icon: FireIcon, label: 'Story Arc' },
  { type: 'parallel', icon: DocumentDuplicateIcon, label: 'Parallel' },
  { type: 'thematic', icon: TrashIcon, label: 'Thematic' },
  { type: 'dialogue', icon: ChatBubbleBottomCenterIcon, label: 'Dialogue' },
];

const ConnectionTypeToolbox: React.FC<ConnectionTypeToolboxProps> = ({ isVisible, onClose }) => {
  const dispatch = useDispatch();
  const [activeTooltip, setActiveTooltip] = useState<string | null>(null);

  if (!isVisible) return null;

  const handleConnectionTypeClick = (type: ConnectionType) => {
    dispatch(setActiveConnectionType(type));
    onClose();
  };

  const handleMouseEnter = (tooltipContent: string) => {
    setActiveTooltip(tooltipContent);
  };

  const handleMouseLeave = () => {
    setActiveTooltip(null);
  };

  return (
    <div className="absolute bottom-full left-0 mb-2 bg-gray-800 rounded-md shadow-lg p-1 flex">
      {connectionTypes.map((ct) => (
        <Tooltip key={ct.type} content={ct.label} visible={activeTooltip === ct.label}>
          <button
            onClick={() => handleConnectionTypeClick(ct.type)}
            onMouseEnter={() => handleMouseEnter(ct.label)}
            onMouseLeave={handleMouseLeave}
            className="p-2 hover:bg-gray-700 rounded-md transition-colors duration-200"
            aria-label={ct.label}
          >
            <ct.icon className="h-5 w-5 text-white" />
          </button>
        </Tooltip>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
};

export default ConnectionTypeToolbox;

================
File: raay/src/components/Playground/Toolbox/FloatingToolbox.tsx
================
// src/components/Playground/Toolbox/FloatingToolbox.tsx

import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { PencilIcon, TrashIcon, LinkIcon, UserPlusIcon, DocumentDuplicateIcon, EyeIcon, ChatBubbleBottomCenterIcon } from '@heroicons/react/24/outline';
import { Tooltip } from '../../Common/Tooltip';
import ConnectionTypeToolbox from './ConnectionTypeToolbox';

interface FloatingToolboxProps {
  onEdit: () => void;
  onDelete: () => void;
  onAddCharacter: () => void;
  onDuplicate: () => void;
  onViewDetails: () => void;
  onAddComment: () => void;
  sceneId: string;
}

const FloatingToolbox: React.FC<FloatingToolboxProps> = ({
  onEdit,
  onDelete,
  onAddCharacter,
  onDuplicate,
  onViewDetails,
  onAddComment,
  sceneId,
}) => {
  const [isConnectionTypeToolboxVisible, setIsConnectionTypeToolboxVisible] = useState(false);
  const [activeTooltip, setActiveTooltip] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const toolboxRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const connectionToolboxRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const timeoutRef = useRef<NodeJS.Timeout | null>(null);

  const toggleConnectionTypeToolbox = () => {
    setIsConnectionTypeToolboxVisible(!isConnectionTypeToolboxVisible);
    setActiveTooltip(null);
  };

  const handleButtonClick = (action: () => void) => {
    action();
    setActiveTooltip(null);
  };

  const handleMouseEnter = (tooltipContent: string) => {
    setActiveTooltip(tooltipContent);
  };

  const handleMouseLeave = () => {
    setActiveTooltip(null);
  };

  const handleToolboxMouseLeave = () => {
    timeoutRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
      setIsConnectionTypeToolboxVisible(false);
      setActiveTooltip(null);
    }, 300); // 300ms delay
  };

  const handleToolboxMouseEnter = () => {
    if (timeoutRef.current) {
      clearTimeout(timeoutRef.current);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleClickOutside = (event: MouseEvent) => {
      if (
        toolboxRef.current &&
        !toolboxRef.current.contains(event.target as Node) &&
        connectionToolboxRef.current &&
        !connectionToolboxRef.current.contains(event.target as Node)
      ) {
        setIsConnectionTypeToolboxVisible(false);
        setActiveTooltip(null);
      }
    };

    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    };
  }, []);

  return (
    <div 
      ref={toolboxRef}
      className="absolute top-0 left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-full mb-2 flex space-x-2 bg-gray-800 bg-opacity-90 rounded-md shadow-lg p-1 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity duration-200"
      onMouseLeave={handleToolboxMouseLeave}
      onMouseEnter={handleToolboxMouseEnter}
    >
      <Tooltip content="Edit Scene" visible={activeTooltip === "Edit Scene"}>
        <button 
          onClick={() => handleButtonClick(onEdit)} 
          onMouseEnter={() => handleMouseEnter("Edit Scene")}
          onMouseLeave={handleMouseLeave}
          className="p-1 hover:bg-gray-700 rounded-md transition-colors duration-200"
        >
          <PencilIcon className="h-5 w-5 text-white" />
        </button>
      </Tooltip>
      <Tooltip content="Delete Scene" visible={activeTooltip === "Delete Scene"}>
        <button 
          onClick={() => handleButtonClick(onDelete)}
          onMouseEnter={() => handleMouseEnter("Delete Scene")}
          onMouseLeave={handleMouseLeave}
          className="p-1 hover:bg-gray-700 rounded-md transition-colors duration-200"
        >
          <TrashIcon className="h-5 w-5 text-white" />
        </button>
      </Tooltip>
      <Tooltip content="Connect Scenes" visible={activeTooltip === "Connect Scenes"}>
        <div className="relative">
          <button
            onClick={toggleConnectionTypeToolbox}
            onMouseEnter={() => handleMouseEnter("Connect Scenes")}
            onMouseLeave={handleMouseLeave}
            className="p-1 hover:bg-gray-700 rounded-md transition-colors duration-200"
          >
            <LinkIcon className="h-5 w-5 text-white" />
          </button>
          <div
            ref={connectionToolboxRef}
            onMouseEnter={handleToolboxMouseEnter}
            onMouseLeave={handleToolboxMouseLeave}
          >
            <ConnectionTypeToolbox
              isVisible={isConnectionTypeToolboxVisible}
              onClose={() => setIsConnectionTypeToolboxVisible(false)}
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      </Tooltip>
      <Tooltip content="Add Character" visible={activeTooltip === "Add Character"}>
        <button 
          onClick={() => handleButtonClick(onAddCharacter)}
          onMouseEnter={() => handleMouseEnter("Add Character")}
          onMouseLeave={handleMouseLeave}
          className="p-1 hover:bg-gray-700 rounded-md transition-colors duration-200"
        >
          <UserPlusIcon className="h-5 w-5 text-white" />
        </button>
      </Tooltip>
      <Tooltip content="Duplicate Scene" visible={activeTooltip === "Duplicate Scene"}>
        <button 
          onClick={() => handleButtonClick(onDuplicate)}
          onMouseEnter={() => handleMouseEnter("Duplicate Scene")}
          onMouseLeave={handleMouseLeave}
          className="p-1 hover:bg-gray-700 rounded-md transition-colors duration-200"
        >
          <DocumentDuplicateIcon className="h-5 w-5 text-white" />
        </button>
      </Tooltip>
      <Tooltip content="View Details" visible={activeTooltip === "View Details"}>
        <button 
          onClick={() => handleButtonClick(onViewDetails)}
          onMouseEnter={() => handleMouseEnter("View Details")}
          onMouseLeave={handleMouseLeave}
          className="p-1 hover:bg-gray-700 rounded-md transition-colors duration-200"
        >
          <EyeIcon className="h-5 w-5 text-white" />
        </button>
      </Tooltip>
      <Tooltip content="Add Comment" visible={activeTooltip === "Add Comment"}>
        <button 
          onClick={() => handleButtonClick(onAddComment)}
          onMouseEnter={() => handleMouseEnter("Add Comment")}
          onMouseLeave={handleMouseLeave}
          className="p-1 hover:bg-gray-700 rounded-md transition-colors duration-200"
        >
          <ChatBubbleBottomCenterIcon className="h-5 w-5 text-white" />
        </button>
      </Tooltip>
    </div>
  );
};

export default FloatingToolbox;

File: raay/src/components/Playground/ConnectionSummary.tsx

// src/components/Playground/ConnectionSummary.tsx

import React, { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import { useSelector, useDispatch } from 'react-redux';
import { RootState } from '../../store';
import { toggleConnectionType } from '../../store/uiSlice';
import { deleteConnection } from '../../store/connectionsSlice';
import { Tooltip } from '../Common/Tooltip';
import { TrashIcon } from '@heroicons/react/24/outline';
import { cn } from '../../lib/utils';
import { FixedSizeList as List, ListChildComponentProps } from 'react-window';

interface ConnectionItemProps {
  connection: {
    from: string;
    to: string;
    type: string;
  };
}

const ConnectionItem: React.FC<ConnectionItemProps> = ({ connection }) => {
  const dispatch = useDispatch();
  const handleDelete = () => {
    dispatch(deleteConnection({ from: connection.from, to: connection.to }));
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex justify-between items-center p-2 hover:bg-gray-200 rounded-md">
      <div>
        <span className="font-semibold">{connection.type.toUpperCase()}</span>:
        <span className="ml-1">Scene {connection.from} → Scene {connection.to}</span>
      </div>
      <Tooltip content="Delete Connection">
        <button onClick={handleDelete} className="text-red-500 hover:text-red-700">
          <TrashIcon className="h-5 w-5" />
        </button>
      </Tooltip>
    </div>
  );
};

const ConnectionsSummary: React.FC = () => {
  const dispatch = useDispatch();
  const connections = useSelector((state: RootState) => state.connections.connections);
  const connectionFilters = useSelector((state: RootState) => state.ui.connectionFilters);
  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState('');

  const filteredConnections = useMemo(() => {
    return connections.filter(
      (conn) =>
        connectionFilters[conn.type as keyof typeof connectionFilters] &&
        (`Scene ${conn.from}`.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase()) ||
          `Scene ${conn.to}`.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase()) ||
          conn.type.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase()))
    );
  }, [connections, connectionFilters, searchTerm]);

  const handleToggleFilter = (type: string) => {
    dispatch(toggleConnectionType(type as keyof typeof connectionFilters));
  };

  return (
    <div className="p-4">
      <h2 className="text-xl font-bold mb-4">All Connections</h2>
      <div className="mb-4">
        <input
          type="text"
          placeholder="Search connections..."
          value={searchTerm}
          onChange={(e) => setSearchTerm(e.target.value)}
          className="w-full p-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-md focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500"
        />
      </div>
      <div className="mb-4">
        <h3 className="font-semibold mb-2">Filter by Type</h3>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {Object.keys(connectionFilters).map((type) => (
            <label key={type} className="flex items-center space-x-1">
              <input
                type="checkbox"
                checked={connectionFilters[type as keyof typeof connectionFilters]}
                onChange={() => handleToggleFilter(type)}
                className="h-4 w-4 text-blue-600 border-gray-300 rounded"
              />
              <span>{type.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + type.slice(1)}</span>
            </label>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="h-64">
        {filteredConnections.length > 0 ? (
          <List
            height={256}
            itemCount={filteredConnections.length}
            itemSize={50}
            width="100%"
            className="border border-gray-300 rounded-md"
          >
            {({ index, style }: ListChildComponentProps) => (
              <div style={style}>
                <ConnectionItem connection={filteredConnections[index]} />
              </div>
            )}
          </List>
        ) : (
          <p className="text-gray-500">No connections found.</p>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default ConnectionsSummary;

import React from 'react'
import { EdgeProps, getBezierPath } from 'reactflow'

const CustomRelationshipEdge: React.FC<EdgeProps> = ({
  id,
  sourceX,
  sourceY,
  targetX,
  targetY,
  sourcePosition,
  targetPosition,
  style,
  markerEnd,

    sourceX,

    sourcePosition,
    targetX,
    targetY,
    targetPosition,

// src/components/Playground/CustomSceneNode.tsx
import React from 'react'
import { Handle, Position } from 'reactflow'
import { useSelector } from 'react-redux'
import { RootState } from '../../store'
import SceneBox from './SceneBox/SceneBox'
import { cn } from '../../lib/utils'

interface CustomSceneNodeProps {
  data: {
    scene: {
      id: string
      title: string
      scriptContent: string
      position: { x: number; y: number }
      size: { width: number; height: number }
      characters: string[]
      color: string
    }
    index: number
  }
}

const CustomSceneNode: React.FC<CustomSceneNodeProps> = ({ data }) => {
  const connectionMode = useSelector((state: RootState) => state.ui.connectionMode)
  const activeConnectionType = useSelector((state: RootState) => state.ui.activeConnectionType)

  const connectionTypes = {
    sequential: '#4f46e5',
    nonLinear: '#f97316',
    reference: '#10b981',
    arc: '#a855f7',
    parallel: '#ef4444',
    thematic: '#6b7280',
    dialogue: '#ec4899',
  }

  return (
    <div className={cn(
      'relative',
      connectionMode && 'ring-2 ring-offset-2',
      connectionMode && activeConnectionType && `ring-${connectionTypes[activeConnectionType as keyof typeof connectionTypes]}`
    )}>
      <Handle type="target" position={Position.Top} />
      <SceneBox data={data} />
      <Handle type="source" position={Position.Bottom} />
    </div>
  )
}

// src/components/Playground/PlaygroundCanvas.tsx

import React, { useCallback, useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useSelector, useDispatch } from 'react-redux';
import { RootState } from '../../store';
import ReactFlow, {
  Node,
  Edge,
  Connection,
  addEdge,
  Background,
  Controls,
  MiniMap,
  useNodesState,
  useEdgesState,
  ConnectionLineType,
} from 'reactflow';
import 'reactflow/dist/style.css';
import { DndProvider } from 'react-dnd';
import { HTML5Backend } from 'react-dnd-html5-backend';
import CustomSceneNode from './CustomSceneNode';
import CustomRelationshipEdge from './CustomRelationshipEdge';
import { addConnection, ConnectionType } from '../../store/connectionsSlice';
import { updateScenePosition } from '../../store/scenesSlice';
import ConnectionSummary from './ConnectionSummary';
import ConnectionSummaryModal from './ConnectionSummaryModal';
import { setConnectionMode, setActiveConnectionType } from '../../store/uiSlice';

const nodeTypes = {
  scene: CustomSceneNode,
};

const edgeTypes = {
  relationship: CustomRelationshipEdge,
};

const PlaygroundCanvas: React.FC = () => {
  const dispatch = useDispatch();
  const scenes = useSelector((state: RootState) => state.scenes.scenes);
  const connections = useSelector((state: RootState) => state.connections.connections);
  const connectionMode = useSelector((state: RootState) => state.ui.connectionMode);
  const activeConnectionType = useSelector((state: RootState) => state.ui.activeConnectionType);

  const [nodes, setNodes, onNodesChange] = useNodesState([]);
  const [edges, setEdges, onEdgesChange] = useEdgesState([]);

  const [isConnectionsModalOpen, setIsConnectionsModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [sourceSceneId, setSourceSceneId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const updatedNodes: Node[] = scenes.map((scene, index) => ({
      id: scene.id,
      type: 'scene',
      data: { scene, index },
      position: scene.position,
      draggable: true,
    }));
    setNodes(updatedNodes);
  }, [scenes, setNodes]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const updatedEdges: Edge[] = connections.map((conn) => ({
      id: `e${conn.from}-${conn.to}`,
      source: conn.from,
      target: conn.to,
      animated: true,
      type: 'relationship',
      data: { connectionType: conn.type, label: conn.type.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + conn.type.slice(1) },
    }));
    setEdges(updatedEdges);
  }, [connections, setEdges]);

  const onConnect = useCallback(
    (params: Connection) => {
      if (connectionMode && activeConnectionType && params.source && params.target) {
        dispatch(addConnection({
          from: params.source,
          to: params.target,
          type: activeConnectionType as ConnectionType,
        }));
        dispatch(setConnectionMode(false));
        dispatch(setActiveConnectionType(null));
      }
    },
    [dispatch, connectionMode, activeConnectionType]
  );

  const onNodeClick = useCallback(
    (event: React.MouseEvent, node: Node) => {
      if (connectionMode && activeConnectionType) {
        if (!sourceSceneId) {
          setSourceSceneId(node.id);
        } else if (node.id !== sourceSceneId) {
          dispatch(addConnection({
            from: sourceSceneId,
            to: node.id,
            type: activeConnectionType as ConnectionType,
          }));
          dispatch(setConnectionMode(false));
          dispatch(setActiveConnectionType(null));
          setSourceSceneId(null);
        }
      }
    },
    [dispatch, connectionMode, activeConnectionType, sourceSceneId]
  );

  const openConnectionsModal = () => {
    setIsConnectionsModalOpen(true);
  };

  const closeConnectionsModal = () => {
    setIsConnectionsModalOpen(false);
  };

  return (
    <>
      <DndProvider backend={HTML5Backend}>
        <div className="h-full">
          <ReactFlow
            nodes={nodes}
            edges={edges}
            onNodesChange={onNodesChange}
            onEdgesChange={onEdgesChange}
            onConnect={onConnect}
            onNodeDragStop={(event, node) => dispatch(updateScenePosition({ id: node.id, position: node.position }))}
            onNodeClick={onNodeClick}
            nodeTypes={nodeTypes}
            edgeTypes={edgeTypes}
            snapToGrid={true}
            snapGrid={[15, 15]}
            fitView
            connectionLineType={ConnectionLineType.SmoothStep}
          >
            <Background color="#999999" gap={15} />
            <Controls />
            <MiniMap />
          </ReactFlow>
        </div>
      </DndProvider>

      <ConnectionSummaryModal isOpen={isConnectionsModalOpen} onClose={closeConnectionsModal} />

      {connectionMode && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black bg-opacity-25 flex items-center justify-center z-50">
          <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-md shadow-lg">
            <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold mb-4">Establishing {activeConnectionType} Connection</h3>
            <p className="mb-4">Click on a scene to {sourceSceneId ? 'complete' : 'start'} the connection.</p>
            <button
              onClick={() => {
                dispatch(setConnectionMode(false));
                dispatch(setActiveConnectionType(null));
                setSourceSceneId(null);
              }}
              className="px-4 py-2 bg-red-600 text-white rounded-md hover:bg-red-700 transition-colors duration-200"
            >
              Cancel
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  );
};

export default PlaygroundCanvas;

// src/components/Playground/ScreenwritingEditor.tsx

"use client"

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react'
import { XMarkIcon, CheckIcon, Bars3Icon } from '@heroicons/react/24/outline'
import Portal from '../UI/Portal' // Import the Portal component

interface ScreenwritingEditorProps {
  scene: {
    id: string
    title: string
    scriptContent: string
  }
  onSave: (updatedScene: { title: string; scriptContent: string }) => void
  onClose: () => void
}

const ScreenwritingEditor: React.FC<ScreenwritingEditorProps> = ({ scene, onSave, onClose }) => {
  const [title, setTitle] = useState(scene.title)
  const [scriptContent, setScriptContent] = useState(scene.scriptContent)
  const editorRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleClickOutside = (event: MouseEvent) => {
      if (editorRef.current && !editorRef.current.contains(event.target as Node)) {
        onClose()
      }
    }

    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside)
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside)
    }
  }, [onClose])

  const handleSave = () => {
    onSave({ title, scriptContent })
  }

  return (
    <Portal>
      <div className="fixed inset-0 flex items-center justify-center z-50 bg-black bg-opacity-50">
        <div 
          ref={editorRef}
          className="bg-gray-100 w-full max-w-4xl h-3/4 rounded-lg shadow-lg flex flex-col"
          style={{
            backgroundImage: 'radial-gradient(circle, #d7d7d7 1px, transparent 1px)',
            backgroundSize: '20px 20px'
          }}
        >
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-4 border-b border-gray-300">
            <button className="text-gray-600 hover:text-gray-800">
              <Bars3Icon className="h-6 w-6" />
            </button>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={title}
              onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)}
              className="text-xl font-bold text-center bg-transparent border-none focus:outline-none focus:ring-0"
              placeholder="Scene Title"
            />
            <div className="flex space-x-4">
              <button
                onClick={handleSave}
                className="text-green-500 hover:text-green-700"
              >
                <CheckIcon className="h-6 w-6" />
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={onClose}
                className="text-red-500 hover:text-red-700"
              >
                <XMarkIcon className="h-6 w-6" />
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="flex-grow p-4 overflow-auto">
            <textarea
              value={scriptContent}
              onChange={(e) => setScriptContent(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full h-full p-4 bg-white rounded-md border border-gray-300 resize-none focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
              placeholder={`EXT. LOCATION - DAY

Character Name
    (parenthetical)
    Dialogue goes here...`}
              style={{
                fontFamily: 'Courier, monospace',
                fontSize: '14px',
                lineHeight: '1.5',
              }}
            />
          </div>

      </div>

// src/components/Playground/ToneMeter.tsx
"use client"

import React from 'react'
import { Progress } from '../Common/Progress'
import { Tooltip } from '../Common/Tooltip'

interface ToneMeterProps {
  currentToneScore: number
  desiredToneScore: number
  toneBreakdown?: {
    category: string
    score: number
  }[]
}

const ToneMeter: React.FC<ToneMeterProps> = ({ currentToneScore, desiredToneScore, toneBreakdown = [] }) => {
  const overallConsistency = (currentToneScore / desiredToneScore) * 100

  return (
    <div className="fixed bottom-0 right-0 p-4 bg-white shadow-lg rounded-tl-lg">
      <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold mb-2">Tone Consistency</h3>
      <div className="mb-2">
        <Progress value={currentToneScore} max={desiredToneScore} />
        <span className="text-sm">{overallConsistency.toFixed(1)}% Consistent</span>
      </div>
      <div className="space-y-1">
        {toneBreakdown.map((item) => (
          <Tooltip key={item.category} content={`${item.category}: ${item.score}%`}>
            <div className="flex items-center">
              <span className="text-xs w-20 truncate">{item.category}</span>
              <Progress value={item.score} max={100} />
            </div>
          </Tooltip>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

export default ToneMeter

"use client"

import React from 'react'
import { useSelector } from 'react-redux'
import { RootState } from '../../store'
import { formatScene } from '../../utils/screenplayFormatter'

const ScreenplayFormatter: React.FC = () => {
  const scenes = useSelector((state: RootState) => state.scenes.scenes)
  const characters = useSelector((state: RootState) => state.characters.characters)

  // Format screenplay content by excluding description
  const formattedScreenplay = scenes.map(scene => {
    const sceneCharacters = characters.filter(char => scene.characters.includes(char.id))
    return formatScene(scene, sceneCharacters)
  }).join('\n\n')

  return (
    <div className="formatted-screenplay p-4 bg-gray-50 rounded-md shadow-md overflow-auto max-h-screen">
      <pre className="whitespace-pre-wrap text-lg">{formattedScreenplay}</pre>
    </div>
  )
}

export default ScreenplayFormatter

// src/components/UI/Portal.tsx

"use client"

import { ReactNode, useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { createPortal } from 'react-dom'

interface PortalProps {
  children: ReactNode
}

const Portal: React.FC<PortalProps> = ({ children }) => {
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false)
  const [container, setContainer] = useState<HTMLElement | null>(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    const portalRoot = document.getElementById('portal-root')
    if (portalRoot) {
      setContainer(portalRoot)
      setMounted(true)
    }
  }, [])

  if (!mounted || !container) return null

  return createPortal(children, container)
}

export default Portal

File: raay/src/components/ErrorBoundary.tsx

// src/components/ErrorBoundary.tsx

"use client"

import React, { Component, ErrorInfo, ReactNode } from 'react'

interface Props {
  children: ReactNode
}

interface State {
  hasError: boolean
}

class ErrorBoundary extends Component<Props, State> {
  state: State = {
    hasError: false,
  }

  static getDerivedStateFromError(_: Error): State {
    return { hasError: true }
  }

  componentDidCatch(error: Error, errorInfo: ErrorInfo) {
    console.error("Uncaught error:", error, errorInfo)
  }

  render() {
    if (this.state.hasError) {
      return <h1 className="text-center mt-20 text-2xl">Something went wrong.</h1>
    }

    return this.props.children
  }
}

export default ErrorBoundary

File: raay/src/context/ModalContext.tsx

"use client"

import React, { createContext, useState, ReactNode, useContext } from 'react'

// Define the shape of the context
interface ModalContextProps {
  isOpen: boolean
  openModal: () => void
  closeModal: () => void
}

// Create the context with default undefined
const ModalContext = createContext<ModalContextProps | undefined>(undefined)

// Create a provider component
export const ModalProvider: React.FC<{ children: ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => {
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false)

  const openModal = () => setIsOpen(true)
  const closeModal = () => setIsOpen(false)

  return (
    <ModalContext.Provider value={{ isOpen, openModal, closeModal }}>
      {children}
    </ModalContext.Provider>
  )
}

// Custom hook for consuming the context
export const useModal = (): ModalContextProps => {
  const context = useContext(ModalContext)
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error('useModal must be used within a ModalProvider')
  }
  return context
}

// src/hooks/useZoomPan.ts

import { useState } from 'react'

const useZoomPan = () => {
  const [zoom, setZoom] = useState(1)
  const [pan, setPan] = useState({ x: 0, y: 0 })

  const zoomIn = () => setZoom(prev => prev + 0.1)
  const zoomOut = () => setZoom(prev => Math.max(prev - 0.1, 0.1))
  const panMove = (x: number, y: number) => setPan({ x, y })

  return { zoom, pan, zoomIn, zoomOut, panMove }
}

export default useZoomPan

// src/store/beatSheetSlice.ts

import { createSlice, PayloadAction } from '@reduxjs/toolkit'

interface Beat {
  id: string
  title: string
  description: string
}

interface BeatSheetState {
  beats: Beat[]
}

const initialState: BeatSheetState = {
  beats: []
}

const beatSheetSlice = createSlice({
  name: 'beatSheet',
  initialState,
  reducers: {
    addBeat: (state, action: PayloadAction<Beat>) => {
      state.beats.push(action.payload)
    },
    updateBeat: (state, action: PayloadAction<Beat>) => {
      const index = state.beats.findIndex(beat => beat.id === action.payload.id)
      if (index !== -1) {
        state.beats[index] = action.payload
      }
    },
    deleteBeat: (state, action: PayloadAction<string>) => {
      state.beats = state.beats.filter(beat => beat.id !== action.payload)
    }
  }
})

export const { addBeat, updateBeat, deleteBeat } = beatSheetSlice.actions
export default beatSheetSlice.reducer

        if (action.payload.description !== undefined) character.description = action.payload.description
      }

    deleteCharacter: (state, action: PayloadAction<string>) => {
      state.characters = state.characters.filter(char => char.id !== action.payload)
    },
    // Define more reducers as needed
  },
})

File: raay/src/store/connectionsSlice.ts

// src/store/connectionsSlice.ts

import { createSlice, PayloadAction } from '@reduxjs/toolkit'

export type ConnectionType = 'sequential' | 'nonLinear' | 'reference' | 'arc' | 'parallel' | 'thematic' | 'dialogue'

interface Connection {
  from: string
  to: string
  type: ConnectionType
}

interface ConnectionsState {
  connections: Connection[]
}

const initialState: ConnectionsState = {
  connections: [],
}

const connectionsSlice = createSlice({
  name: 'connections',
  initialState,
  reducers: {
    addConnection: (state, action: PayloadAction<Connection>) => {
      // Prevent duplicate connections
      const exists = state.connections.some(
        (conn) => conn.from === action.payload.from && conn.to === action.payload.to
      )
      if (!exists) {
        state.connections.push(action.payload)
      }
    },
    deleteConnection: (state, action: PayloadAction<{ from: string; to: string }>) => {
      state.connections = state.connections.filter(
        (conn) => !(conn.from === action.payload.from && conn.to === action.payload.to)
      )
    },
    // Add more reducers if needed
  },
})

export const { addConnection, deleteConnection } = connectionsSlice.actions
export default connectionsSlice.reducer

// src/store/propsSlice.ts

import { createSlice, PayloadAction } from '@reduxjs/toolkit'

interface Prop {
  id: string
  name: string
  description: string
}

interface PropsState {
  props: Prop[]
}

const initialState: PropsState = {
  props: [],
}

const propsSlice = createSlice({
  name: 'props',
  initialState,
  reducers: {
    addProp: (state, action: PayloadAction<Prop>) => {
      state.props.push(action.payload)
    },
    updateProp: (state, action: PayloadAction<{ id: string; name?: string; description?: string }>) => {
      const prop = state.props.find(p => p.id === action.payload.id)
      if (prop) {
        if (action.payload.name !== undefined) prop.name = action.payload.name
        if (action.payload.description !== undefined) prop.description = action.payload.description
      }
    },
    deleteProp: (state, action: PayloadAction<string>) => {
      state.props = state.props.filter(p => p.id !== action.payload)
    },
    // Define more reducers as needed
  },
})

export const { addProp, updateProp, deleteProp } = propsSlice.actions
export default propsSlice.reducer

File: raay/src/store/scenesSlice.ts
================
// src/store/scenesSlice.ts

import { createSlice, PayloadAction, createAsyncThunk } from '@reduxjs/toolkit'

interface Scene {
  id: string
  title: string
  scriptContent: string
  position: { x: number; y: number }
  size: { width: number; height: number }
  characters: string[]
  color: string
}

interface ScenesState {
  scenes: Scene[]
}

const initialState: ScenesState = {
  scenes: [],
}

// Async action for updating scene
export const updateSceneAsync = createAsyncThunk(
  'scenes/updateScene',
  async (updatedScene: { id: string; title: string; scriptContent: string }, thunkAPI) => {
    // Simulate API call or perform actual API integration here
    return new Promise<{ id: string; title: string; scriptContent: string }>((resolve) => {
      setTimeout(() => {
        resolve(updatedScene)
      }, 500)
    })
  }
)

const scenesSlice = createSlice({
  name: 'scenes',
  initialState,
  reducers: {
    addScene: (state, action: PayloadAction<Scene>) => {
      state.scenes.push(action.payload)
    },
    updateScenePosition: (
      state,
      action: PayloadAction<{ id: string; position: { x: number; y: number } }>
    ) => {
      const scene = state.scenes.find(scene => scene.id === action.payload.id)
      if (scene) {
        scene.position = action.payload.position
      }
    },
    updateScene: (
      state,
      action: PayloadAction<{ id: string; title: string; scriptContent: string }>
    ) => {
      const scene = state.scenes.find(scene => scene.id === action.payload.id)
      if (scene) {
        scene.title = action.payload.title
        scene.scriptContent = action.payload.scriptContent
      }
    },
    assignCharacterToScene: (
      state,
      action: PayloadAction<{ sceneId: string; characterId: string }>
    ) => {
      const scene = state.scenes.find(scene => scene.id === action.payload.sceneId)
      if (scene && !scene.characters.includes(action.payload.characterId)) {
        scene.characters.push(action.payload.characterId)
      }
    },
    removeCharacterFromScene: (
      state,
      action: PayloadAction<{ sceneId: string; characterId: string }>
    ) => {
      const scene = state.scenes.find(scene => scene.id === action.payload.sceneId)
      if (scene) {
        scene.characters = scene.characters.filter(id => id !== action.payload.characterId)
      }
    },
    deleteScene: (state, action: PayloadAction<string>) => {
      state.scenes = state.scenes.filter(scene => scene.id !== action.payload)
    },
    reorderScenes: (state, action: PayloadAction<string[]>) => {
      const newOrder = action.payload
      state.scenes = newOrder.map(id => state.scenes.find(scene => scene.id === id)!).filter(Boolean)
    },
  },
  extraReducers: (builder) => {
    builder.addCase(updateSceneAsync.fulfilled, (state, action) => {
      const scene = state.scenes.find(scene => scene.id === action.payload.id)
      if (scene) {
        scene.title = action.payload.title
        scene.scriptContent = action.payload.scriptContent
      }
    })
  },
})

export const {
  addScene,
  updateScenePosition,
  updateScene,
  assignCharacterToScene,
  removeCharacterFromScene,
  deleteScene,
  reorderScenes,
} = scenesSlice.actions

export default scenesSlice.reducer

================
File: raay/src/store/uiSlice.ts
================
// src/store/uiSlice.ts

import { createSlice, PayloadAction } from '@reduxjs/toolkit';

type ConnectionType =
  | 'sequential'
  | 'nonLinear'
  | 'reference'
  | 'arc'
  | 'parallel'
  | 'thematic'
  | 'dialogue'
  | null;

interface UiState {
  connectionFilters: {
    [key in Exclude<ConnectionType, null>]: boolean;
  };
  connectionMode: boolean;
  activeConnectionType: ConnectionType;
}

const initialState: UiState = {
  connectionFilters: {
    sequential: true,
    nonLinear: true,
    reference: true,
    arc: true,
    parallel: true,
    thematic: true,
    dialogue: true,
  },
  connectionMode: false,
  activeConnectionType: null,
};

const uiSlice = createSlice({
  name: 'ui',
  initialState,
  reducers: {
    toggleConnectionType: (state, action: PayloadAction<Exclude<ConnectionType, null>>) => {
      state.connectionFilters[action.payload] = !state.connectionFilters[action.payload];
    },
    resetConnectionFilters: (state) => {
      Object.keys(state.connectionFilters).forEach((key) => {
        state.connectionFilters[key as Exclude<ConnectionType, null>] = true;
      });
    },
    setConnectionMode: (state, action: PayloadAction<boolean>) => {
      state.connectionMode = action.payload;
      if (!action.payload) {
        state.activeConnectionType = null;
      }
    },
    setActiveConnectionType: (state, action: PayloadAction<ConnectionType>) => {
      state.activeConnectionType = action.payload;
      if (action.payload) {
        state.connectionMode = true;
      }
    },
  },
});

export const {
  toggleConnectionType,
  resetConnectionFilters,
  setConnectionMode,
  setActiveConnectionType,
} = uiSlice.actions;

export default uiSlice.reducer;

export function cn(...classes: (string | undefined | null | false)[]) {

}

File: raay/src/utils/screenplayFormatter.ts
================
// src/utils/screenplayFormatter.ts

interface Scene {
  id: string
  title: string
  scriptContent: string
  position: { x: number; y: number }
  size: { width: number; height: number }
  characters: string[]
  color: string
}

interface Character {
  id: string
  name: string
  description: string
}

export const formatScene = (scene: Scene, characters: Character[]): string => {
  const sceneNumberMatch = scene.title.match(/Scene (\d+)/i)
  const sceneNumber = sceneNumberMatch ? sceneNumberMatch[1] : 'Unknown'
  const location = extractLocation(scene.title)
  const timeOfDay = extractTimeOfDay(scene.title)

  const sceneHeading = `EXT. ${location} - ${timeOfDay}`

  const formattedScript = scriptContentToFormattedText(scene.scriptContent, characters)

  return `${sceneHeading}\n\n${formattedScript}`
}

const extractLocation = (title: string): string => {
  // Example: "Scene 1: Office" => "OFFICE"
  const match = title.match(/Scene \d+: (.+)/i)
  return match ? match[1].toUpperCase() : "UNKNOWN LOCATION"
}

const extractTimeOfDay = (title: string): string => {
  // Example: "Scene 1: Office (Day)" => "DAY"
  const match = title.match(/\((Day|Night|Evening|Morning)\)/i)
  return match ? match[1].toUpperCase() : "DAY"
}

const scriptContentToFormattedText = (scriptContent: string, characters: Character[]): string => {
  // Basic formatter: Replace character IDs with names
  // Implement more sophisticated formatting as needed
  let formatted = scriptContent

  characters.forEach(char => {
    const regex = new RegExp(`\\b${char.name}\\b`, 'g')
    formatted = formatted.replace(regex, `**${char.name.toUpperCase()}**`)
  })

  return formatted
}

File: raay/README.md
================
This is a [Next.js](https://nextjs.org) project bootstrapped with [`create-next-app`](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/api-reference/cli/create-next-app).

## Getting Started

First, run the development server:

```bash
npm run dev
# or
yarn dev
# or
pnpm dev
# or
bun dev
```

Open [http://localhost:3000](http://localhost:3000) with your browser to see the result.

You can start editing the page by modifying `app/page.tsx`. The page auto-updates as you edit the file.

This project uses [`next/font`](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/optimizing/fonts) to automatically optimize and load [Geist](https://vercel.com/font), a new font family for Vercel.

## Learn More

To learn more about Next.js, take a look at the following resources:

- [Next.js Documentation](https://nextjs.org/docs) - learn about Next.js features and API.
- [Learn Next.js](https://nextjs.org/learn) - an interactive Next.js tutorial.

## Deploy on Vercel

The easiest way to deploy your Next.js app is to use the [Vercel Platform](https://vercel.com/new?utm_medium=default-template&filter=next.js&utm_source=create-next-app&utm_campaign=create-next-app-readme) from the creators of Next.js.

Check out our [Next.js deployment documentation](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/deploying) for more details.

File: raay/tailwind.config.ts

import type { Config } from "tailwindcss"

const config: Config = {
  darkMode: ["class"],
  content: [
    "./src/pages/**/*.{js,ts,jsx,tsx,mdx}",
    "./src/components/**/*.{js,ts,jsx,tsx,mdx}",
    "./src/app/**/*.{js,ts,jsx,tsx,mdx}",
  ],
  theme: {
    extend: {
      colors: {
        background: 'var(--background)',
        foreground: 'var(--foreground)',
        primary: {
          DEFAULT: '#4f46e5', // Indigo-600
        },
        secondary: {
          DEFAULT: '#9ca3af', // Gray-400
        },
      },
      borderRadius: {
        lg: 'var(--radius)',
        md: 'calc(var(--radius) - 2px)',
        sm: 'calc(var(--radius) - 4px)',
      },
      transitionProperty: {
        'height': 'height',
        'spacing': 'margin, padding',
      },
      animation: {
        'fade-in': 'fadeIn 0.5s ease-in-out',
        'fade-out': 'fadeOut 0.5s ease-in-out',
      },
      keyframes: {
        fadeIn: {
          '0%': { opacity: '0' },
          '100%': { opacity: '1' },
        },
        fadeOut: {
          '0%': { opacity: '1' },
          '100%': { opacity: '0' },
        },
      },
      backgroundSize: {
        '15': '15px',
        '30': '30px',
      },
      spacing: {
        '15': '15px',
        '30': '30px',
      },
      backdropFilter: {
        'none': 'none',
        'blur': 'blur(20px)',
      },
      fontFamily: {
        mono: ['Courier New', 'Courier', 'monospace'],
      },
    },
  },
  plugins: [
    require("@tailwindcss/forms"),
    require("tailwindcss-animate"),
    require('tailwindcss-filters'),
  ],
}

export default config
